5:51:415 hours, 51 minutes, 41 secondsdistribution of those stains. With looking at th that information, we can tell you the how, what, when, where, and
5:51:495 hours, 51 minutes, 49 secondshow things were created by looking at those stains. um that information, the size, shape, and distribution will answer some of those questions.
5:51:585 hours, 51 minutes, 58 secondsCan you tell the direction um of a blood stain pattern? You sometimes can. Yes.
5:52:045 hours, 52 minutes, 4 secondsAnd and how how do you go about doing that? And why is that important?
5:52:085 hours, 52 minutes, 8 secondsSo, in some stains, there is an identifying um feature that we look for.
5:52:125 hours, 52 minutes, 12 secondsIt's called a tail. So when blood flies through the air and lands on a surface, it usually will land in an oval shape um
5:52:215 hours, 52 minutes, 21 secondsan elliptical shape uh that may cause it to have a tail. The tail is an extension of that elliptical shape. We use that to
5:52:305 hours, 52 minutes, 30 secondsidentify the direction that the blood is traveling. So the tail will point in the direction that the blood was flying when it landed on a surface. So we can tell
5:52:395 hours, 52 minutes, 39 secondsby that tail where that stain may have originated from.
5:52:435 hours, 52 minutes, 43 secondsAnd um are there different categories of stains? There are. And what would those be?
5:52:485 hours, 52 minutes, 48 secondsUm we describe them as passive stains, transfer stains, and dynamic stains. And could you define what those are?
5:52:555 hours, 52 minutes, 55 secondsSure. So passive stains are stains where only the force of gravity is acting upon that stain. Um some of the examples
5:53:045 hours, 53 minutes, 4 secondswould be a drip stain, a flow or a blood pool. We have transfer stains which are stains that are created when a bloody
5:53:125 hours, 53 minutes, 12 secondsobject or body comes into contact with another surface transferring that stain there. Um we have for that we have
5:53:205 hours, 53 minutes, 20 secondssmears or wipes. Um and then for dynamic stains, dynamic stains are where a
